## `AGO-1050-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Mycotoxin Specialist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Dairy Product Aflatoxin M1 & Antibiotic HPLC Lab
- **Intro:** Mycotoxin Specialist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Dairy Product Aflatoxin M1 & Antibiotic HPLC Lab — Pre-Startup System Verification: Column oven: 30.0±0.5°C; Flow rate: 1.00±0.02 mL/min; Pressure: 150–250 bar; Mobile phase composition: ±0.5% absolute.
- **Key tolerance:** Column oven: 30.0±0.5°C; Flow rate: 1.00±0.02 mL/min; Pressure: 150–250 bar; Mobile phase composition: ±0.5% absolute.
- **Standards:** `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`, `Method 2000.68`, `Regulation 1881`, `NPA 1234:2018`

- **Role:** Mycotoxin Specialist
- **Scenario:** Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Dairy Product Aflatoxin M1 & Antibiotic HPLC Lab
